1b1(sher phyin): @#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya pra dz+nyA pA ra mi tAs ny+tsa ya gA thA/bod skad du/'phags pa shes rab kyi pha 
1b2(sher phyin): rol du phyin pa sdud pa tshigs su bcad pa/'phags pa 'jam dpal la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das 
1b3(sher phyin): kyis 'khor bzhi po de dag yang dag par rab tu dga' bar mdzad pa'i phyir yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ston cing /
1b4(sher phyin): de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//dga' dang gus dang dad pa'i mchog ni nyer bzhag ste//sgrib pa 
1b5(sher phyin): nyon mongs bsal nas dri ma las 'das pa//'gro don mngon zhugs nges pa'i shes rab pha rol phyin//gang la dpal 
1b6(sher phyin): rnams spyod pa de ni mnyan par gyis//'dzam bu'i gling 'dir chu klung ji snyed cig 'bab cing //me tog 'bras ldan sman dang 
1b7(sher phyin): nags tshal skyed byed pa//ma dros gnas pa'i klu dbang klu bdag brten gnas te//de ni klu yi bdag po de yi mthu dpal yin//
1b8(sher phyin): rgyal ba'i nyan thos pa dag ji snyed chos ston dang //'chad dang rigs pa dag dang ldan par brjod pa dang //mchog 
2a1(sher phyin): @#//'phags bde ba byed dang de yi 'bras thob pa//de dag kun kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i skyes bu'i mthu//ci phyir zhen rgyal bas chos tshul gang bstan pa//de la mi mchog slob mar gyur pas mngon bslabs 
2a2(sher phyin): shing /mngon sum byas nas bslabs pa ji bzhin ston byed de//sangs rgyas mthus byed rang gi stobs kyi mthus ma yin//gang la shes rab pha rol phyin mchog mi dmigs shing //byang chub sems dpa' mi dmigs 
2a3(sher phyin): byang chub sems mi dmigs//de skad thos nas rmongs pa med cing skrag med pa//byang chub sems dpa' de ni bde gshegs shes rab spyod//gzugs med tshor ba med cing 'du shes sems dpa' med//de la rnam 
2a4(sher phyin): shes gnas ni rdul tsam yod ma yin//de chos kun la mi gnas gnas pa med par spyod//yongs su gzung med bde gshegs rnams kyi byang chub 'thob//ji ltar kun du rgyu ba bzo sbyangs shes pa yis//dmigs 
2a5(sher phyin): med phung po rnam par 'jig pa 'byung ba ltar//byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig de ltar chos shes pa//mya ngan 'das la myi rag de ni shes rab gnas//yang 'di shes rab 'di ni gang yin su yi yin//gang las yin zhes 
2a6(sher phyin): chos 'di thams cad stong par rtog/nye bar rtags nas zhum pa med cing skrag med pa////byang chub sems dpa' de na byang chub nye ba yin//gal te mi shes bzhin du gzugs su 'du shes shing //tshor ba sems pa rnam shes phung por 
2a7(sher phyin): spyod byed cing //phung 'di stong zhes rtog na'ang byang chub sems dpa' ni//mtshan ma la spyod skye med gnas la dad ma yin//gang zhig gzugs min tshor min 'du shes sems pa min//rnam par shes la mi spyod gnas pa med par 
2a8(sher phyin): spyod//de ni spyod ces bya bar mi dmigs shes rab brtan//skye med blo ldan ting 'dzin zhi ba mchog la reg/byang chub sems gang de ltar 'dir bdag zhir gnas pa//de na sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pas lung bstan yin//bdag ni mnyam 
2b1(sher phyin): par bzhag ce'am langs shes rlom sems med//ci phyir zhen chos kyi rang bzhin yongs shes phyir//de ltar spyod na bde gshegs rnams kyi shes rab spyod//des ni spyod med spyod pa yin par rab shes phyir//chos gang spyod pa de yang 
2b2(sher phyin): dmigs par mi 'gyur te//'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//gang zhig yod pa ma yin de ni med ces bya//byis pa rnams kyis de brtags yod par med par byed//yod dang med pa 'di gnyis med pa'i chos yin te//
2b3(sher phyin): byang chub sems dpa' gang gis 'di shes nges par 'byung //gang 'dir phung po lnga dag sgyu ma 'drar shes shing //sgyu ma gzhan dang phung po gzhan du mi byed la//sna tshogs 'du shes bral zhing nye bar zhi spyod pa//'di ni shes rab 
2b4(sher phyin): pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa min//dge ba'i bshes dang ldan zhing lhag mthong ldan gyur pa//rgyal ba rnams kyi yum thos skrag par yod mi 'gyur//gang zhig sdig pa'i grogs ldan gzhan gyi dring 'jog pa//de ni snod so ma 
2b5(sher phyin): btang chus reg zhig pa bzhin//ci phyir 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' bya zhe na//chags pa gcod byed kun la chags pa zad par 'dod//rgyal ba rnams kyi byang chub chags med gyur la reg/des na 'di ni byang chub sems pa'i 
2b6(sher phyin): ming thob bo//ci phyir 'di ni sems dpa' che zhes bya zhe na//sems can tshogs rnams mang po'i mchog tu gyur pa dang //sems can khams kyi lta rnams chen po gcod pa ste//de phyir sems dpa' chen po zhes ni rab brjod 
2b7(sher phyin): do//gtong ba che dang blo che ba dang mthu che dang //rgyal ba rnams kyi theg chen mchog la zhugs pa dang //go cha chen po dgos shing bdud kyi sgyu 'dul ba//de yi phyir na sems dpa' che zhes rab tu brjod//dper na sgyu ma 
2b8(sher phyin): mkhan gyis bzhi mdor sprul byas te//skye bo phal chen mgo mang bye ba gcod byed pa'i//bsod bya de dag ci 'dra de ltar byang chub sems//'gro kun sprul 'dra rab shes de la 'jigs pa med//gzugs dang 'du shes tshor 
3a1(sher phyin): @#//
3b1(sher phyin): med de ltar spyod//shes rab ldan te mnyam nyid gnas par shes par bya//chos rnams rang bzhin med par yongs su shes gyur pa//'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//gang zhig gzugs la mi gnas 
3b2(sher phyin): tshor la mi gnas shing //gang zhig 'du shes mi gnas sems par mi gnas la//rnam shes gang mi gnas pa chos nyid gnas pa ste//'di ni shes rab pha rolo phyin mchog spyod pa yin/rtag dang mi rtag 
3b3(sher phyin): bde dang sdug bsngal sdug mi sdug/bdag dang bdag med de bzhin nyid dang de bzhin stong //'bras bu thob dang dgra bcom sa la mi gnas shing //rang rgyal sa dang de bzhin sangs rgyas sar mi gnas/ji ltar 'dren pa 'dus mi byas khams mi gnas shing de bzhin 
3b4(sher phyin): 'dus byas la yang mi gnas gnas med spyod//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' gnas la gnas med gnas//gnas med gnas yin de ni rgyal bas gnas par gsungs//gang dag bde gshegs nyan thos 
3b5(sher phyin): 'gyur bar bya snyam dang //rang sangs rgyas dang de bzhin chos rgyal 'gyur 'dod pa//bzod pa 'di la mi rten thob par mi nus te//dper na tshu rol pha rol 'gro dogs mi mthong bzhin//chos 'chad pa dang nyan dang 
3b6(sher phyin): bshad bya gang yin dang //'bras bu thob dang rkyen rgyal de bzhin 'jig rten mgon//mkhas pa gsal bas thob pa'i mya ngan 'das gang yin//de kun sgyu ma lta bur de bzhin gshegs pas bstan//gang zag bzhi 
3b7(sher phyin): po 'di dag de la mi skrag ste//rgyal sras bden la mkhas dang phyir mi ldog pa dang //dgra bcom dri ma bsal zhing som nyi spangs pa dang //bzhi pa dge ba'i bshes kyis yongs su zin pa'o//byang chub sems dpa' mkhas 
3b8(sher phyin): rig de ltar spyod pa ni//dgra bcom sa la mi slob rkyen rtogs sa la min//thams cad mkhyen nyid ched du sangs rgyas chos rjes slob//gang zhig slob mi slob la mi slob de slob yin//gzugs 'phel yongs su nyams dang 
4a1(sher phyin): @#//
4b1(sher phyin): gshegs pa'i mchod rten de dag gang 'ga'i//bye ma snyed kyis bye ba phrag stong zhing bkang ste//yongs su brtag bzung zhing rnams bye ba mtha' yas na//sems can ji snyed 'khod pa de dag thams cad 
4b2(sher phyin): kyis//las gzhan mi byed par lha'i me tog spos rab dang //byug pas dus gsum bskal pa'am de bas lhag mchod la//gang zhig gang las rnam 'dren stobs bcu 'byung 'gyur ba//bde bar gshegs kyi 
4b3(sher phyin): yum 'di glegs bam la bris te//'chang zhing me tog byug pas sti stang byed gyur na//mchod rten byas mchod bsod nams des ni char mi phod//rgyal ba'i shes rab pha rol phyin 'di rigs sngags che//
4b4(sher phyin): sems can khams mang mya ngan sdug bsngal chos zhi byed//gang dag 'das dang gang dag phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten mgon//rig sngags 'di bslabs sman pa'i rgyal po bla med gyur//gang dag phan dang snying rjer 
4b5(sher phyin): bcas pa spyad spyod pa//rig sngags 'di la bslabs nas mkhas pa byang chub reg/'dus byas bde dang 'dus ma byas bde gang yin pa//bde ba de kun 'di las byung bar rig par bya//sa bon btab nas sa 
4b6(sher phyin): la gnas te 'byung 'gyur ba//tshogs pa snyed nas rnams mang gzugs dag rnam par skye//pha rol phyin lnga byang chub yon tan ji snyed pa//de dag kun kyang shes rab pha rol phyin las skye//'khor los sgyur 
4b7(sher phyin): rgyal lam gang nas ni rtag 'gro ba//lam de nyid nas rin chen bdun dang dpung tshogs kun//gang nas rgyal ba'i shes rab pha rol phyin 'di 'gro//
4b8(sher phyin): de nyid nas ni yon tan chos kun 'gro bar 'gyur//rgyal bas brgya byin dri ba yongs dris lan gsol ba//gal te gang ga'i bye snyed sangs rgyas zhing gyur la//de kun rgyal ba'i ring bsrel byur bur bltam gyur kyang //
5a1(sher phyin): bdag ni shes rab pha rol tu phyin pa 'di nyid len//ci slad ces na sku gdung mi gus bdag ma lags//'on kyang shes rab yongs su bsgos pas mchod par bgyid//dper na rgyal po rten mi rim 'gro 
5a2(sher phyin): thob pa ltar//sangs rgyas ring bsrel shes rab pha rol phyin la rten//nor bu rin chen yon tan kun ldan rin thang myed//gab tshe gang du brtsal ba de ni phyag bgyi 'os//de phyung gyur kyang gab tshe la ni dga' bgyid de//
5a3(sher phyin): yon tan de dag nor bu rin chen de'i lags//de bzhin shes rab pha rol phyin mchog yon tan gyis//rgyal ba mya ngan 'das kyang ring bsrel mchod pa rnyed//de ltas gang zhig rgyal ba'i yon tan 'dzin 'tshal des//shes 
5a4(sher phyin): rab pha rol phyin blang 'di ni thar pa lags//sbyin pa sbyin pa'i mngon du 'gro ba shes rab ste//tshul khrims bzod pa brtson 'grus bsam gtan de bzhin no//dge chos chud mi zan phyir yongs su 'dzin pa yin//
5a5(sher phyin): 'di ni chos rnams thams cad tshul ci ston par byed//dper na 'dzam bu gling na shing rnams bye ba stong //rnam pa sna tshogs rnam pa du ma gzugs mang ba//grib mar gyur pa'i grangs su brjod pa ma gtogs par//
5a6(sher phyin): grib ma sna tshogs med cing bye brag yod ma yin//de bzhin rgyal ba'i pha rol phyin lnga 'di dag kyang //shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i ming nyid thob par 'gyur//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid phyir yongs su bsngos byas na//
5a7(sher phyin): byang chub ces byar drug po 'di kun ro gcig 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa' gal te rab tu mi shes bzhin//gzugs dang 'du shes tshor dang sems pa rnam par shes//mi rtag yongs su ston pa chos la spyod 
5a8(sher phyin): pa ste//mkhas pa rnam yang chos rnams 'jig par mi byed do//gang la gzugs min tshor min 'du shes mi dmigs shing //rnam par shes pa ma yin sems pa mi dmigs la//chos kun skye myed 
5b1(sher phyin): stong pa'i tshul du rab shes pa//'di ni shes rab kyi pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//la las gang ga'i klung gi bye snyed zhing rnams na//sems can ji snyed 'khod kun dgra bcom nyid btul bas//
5b2(sher phyin): gang gis shes rab pha rol phyin 'di yig bris te//sems can mchog la glegs byin bsod nams khyad par 'phags//ci phyir zhe na smra mchog de dag 'dir brlabs nas//chos rnams mtha' 
5b3(sher phyin): dag stong pa nyid du 'dir ston byed//de thos nyan thos myur du rnam par grol la reg/rang rgyal byang chub dang ni sangs rgyas byang chub reg/myu gu med par 'jig rten sdong 'byung yod med na//
5b4(sher phyin): de la yal ma dab me tog 'bras bu ga la 'byung //byang chub sems med rgyal 'byung 'jig rten yod med na//'bras bu brgya byin tshangs pa nyan thos ga la 'byung //nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gang tshe 'od 
5b5(sher phyin): kyi dra ba 'gyed//de tshe sems can las byed brtson pa de bzhin du//mkhas pa shes phyir 'jig rten byang chub sems byung na//ye shes kyis ni sems can yon tan chos dang ldan//dper na 
5b6(sher phyin): ma dros mtsho la klu bdag med gyur na//'dzam bu'i gling 'dir chu klung 'bab par ga la 'gyur//chu klung med na me tog 'bras bu 'byung mi 'gyur//rgya mtsho la yang rin chen gzugs mang 
5b7(sher phyin): med par 'gyur//de bzhin 'dir yang byang chub sems med bde gshegs kyi//ye shes 'jig rten 'di dag kun du ga la 'byung //ye shes med na yon tan 'phel med byang chub med//
5b8(sher phyin): rgya mtsho 'dra ba'i sangs rgyas chos kyang med par 'gyur//'jig rten 'di na snang byed srog chags ji snyed pa//kun gyis snang bar bya phyir 'od rnams 'phyung ba pas//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'od 
6a1(sher phyin): @#//zer gcig phyung ches mchog ste//snang byed tshogs kyi 'od rnams kun gyis char mi phod//nyan thos tshogs rnams ji snyed sbyin dang tshul khrims dang //bsgom dang rab tu ldan pa'i bsod nams 
6a2(sher phyin): tshogs bskyed pa 'ang //byang chub sems dpa' rjes su yid rang sems gcig la//nyan thos tshogs kyi bsod nams phung pos char mi phod//'das dus sngon gyi sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig gang //
6a3(sher phyin): zhing mang mtha' yas bye stong dag na gang bzhugs dang //gang yang mya ngan 'das pa 'jig rten mgon po rnams//sdug bsngal zad par bya phyir rin chen chos ston pa//dang po byang chub mchog tu 
6a4(sher phyin): thugs bskyed bskyed pa nas//rnam par 'dren pa rnams kyi dam chos zad kyi dus//bde bar rgyal ba de dag bsod nams gang yin pa//pha rol phyin dang ldan pa sangs rgyas chos gang dang //
6a5(sher phyin): gang yang sangs rgyas sras rnams dang ni nyan thos dang //slob dang mi slob dge ba zag bcas zag med pa//bsdus nas byang chub sems dpa' rjes yid rang zhing //'gro ba'i don rgyu byang chub phyir ni 
6a6(sher phyin): thams cad bsngo//yongs bsngo gal te sems su 'du shes 'byung 'gyur zhing //byang chub 'du shes yongs bsngo sems can 'du shes na//'du shes phyir ni lta gnas sems ni gsum la chags//
6a7(sher phyin): dmigs pa yod pas yongs su bsngo bar chud ma yin//gal te 'di ltar chos 'di 'gags shing zad pa dang //gang du yongs su bsngo ba de yang zad pa dang //chos kyis chos la nam yang mi 
6a8(sher phyin): bsngo shes gyur na//de ltar rab tu shes pas yongs su bsngos pa yin//gal te mtshan mar byed na de ni bsngo ma yin//ji ste mtshan ma med na byang chub bsngo ba yin//ji ltar dug dang 'dres 
6b1(sher phyin): pa'i kha zas bzang za ba//dkar po'i chos la dmigs pa'ang de 'drar rgyal bas gsungs//de ltas 'di ltar yongs su bsngo ba bslab bya ste//ji ltar rgyal ba de dag dge ba de mkhyen pa'i//rnam 
6b2(sher phyin): pa gang yin 'byung gang mtshan nyid gang yin la//rjes su yid rang de lta de ltar yongs su bsngo//de ltar bsod nams byang chub yongs su bsngo byed na//dug med sangs rgyas mi spong rgyal bas 
6b3(sher phyin): gsungs pa smra//de ltar yongs bsngo dpa' bo 'jig rten dmigs can gyi//byang chub sems dpa' ji snyed thams cad zil gyis gnon//dmus long dmigs bu med pa bye ba khrag khrig rnams//
6b4(sher phyin): lam yang mi shes grong khyer 'jug par ga la 'gyur//shes rab med na mig med pha rol phyin lnga 'di//dmigs bu med pas byang chub reg par nus ma yin//gang tshe shes rab kyis ni rab tu zin 
6b5(sher phyin): gyur na//de tshe mig rnyed gyur cing 'di yang mid thob bo//dper na ri mo'i las ni yongs zin mig med pa//ji srid mig ma bris pas rngan pa mi thob bzhin//gang tshe 'dus byas 'dus ma byas dang 
6b6(sher phyin): dkar nag chos//shes rab rnam par bshig nas rdul tsam mi dmigs tshe//'jig rten dag na shes rab pha rol phyin grangs 'gro//nam mkha' gang la'ang cung zad mi gnas de dang 'dra//gal te bdag 
6b7(sher phyin): ni rgyal ba'i shes rab spyad bya zhing //sems can sdug bsngal reg pa khrag khrig mang dgrol sems//sems can 'du shes yongs rtog byang chub sems dpa' ste//'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog 
6b8(sher phyin): spyod ma yin//byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig sngon chad spyod pa'i tshe//pha rol phyin 'dir spyod pa mkhas pa som nyi med//thos ma thag du des ni ston pa'i 'du shes bskyed//des ni byang chub zhi ba 
7a1(sher phyin): @#//myur du rtogs par 'gyur//sngon spyod tshe na sangs rgyas khrag khrig bkur byas kyang //rgyal ba'i shes rab pha rol phyin la ma dad na//thos nas blo chung de yis 'di ni spong byed de//spangs nas 
7a2(sher phyin): skyabs med 'gyur ba de ni mnar med 'gro//de lta bas na gal te sangs rgyas ye shes mchog/reg par 'dod na rgyal ba'i yum 'dir dad par bgyis//dper na tshong pa rin chen gling du song nas 
7a3(sher phyin): ni/zong zad slar log lta bur gyur na mi rung ngo //gzugs kyi dag pa 'bras bu dag par rig par bya//'bras bu gzugs dag thams cad mkhyen nyid dag par 'gyur//thams cad mkhyen nyid 'bras 
7a4(sher phyin): bu dag dang gzugs dag pa//nam mkha'i khams dang mtshungs te dbyer med gcad du med//dpa' bo gar spyod shes rab pha rol phyin pa yis//khams gsum yang dag 'das la rnam par grol 
7a5(sher phyin): ba'ang yin//nyon mongs bsal bar gyur kyang skye ba ston par byed//dang na dang 'chi ba med kyang 'chi 'pho ston//'gro ba 'di dag ming dang gzugs kyi 'dam la chags//rlung gi 'khor lo 
7a6(sher phyin): lta bu'i 'khor ba'i 'khor lor 'khyam//'gro ba 'khrul pa ri dgas rgyar chud 'drar shes nas//shes rab ldan pa mkha' la bya bzhin rnam par rgyu//spyod pa yongs dag gang zhig gzugs la mi spyod 
7a7(sher phyin): cing /rnam shes 'du shes tshor ba sems par mi spyod na//de ltar spyod pa chags pa thams cad yongs spong zhing //chags las rnam grol bde gshegs rnams kyi shes rab spyod//de ltar spyod pa'i 
7a8(sher phyin): byang chub sems dpa' mkhas gsal ba//chags pa bcad nas 'gro la chags pa med par 'gro//nyi ma sgra gcan gza' bral lha der 'dug pa dang //me btang rtswa dang shing dang nags tshal sreg pa bzhin//
7b1(sher phyin): chos rnams thams cad rang bzhin dag cing yongs dag par//byang chub sems dpa' shes rab pha rol phyin lta na//byed pa po yang mi dmigs chos kun mi dmigs te//'di ni shes 
7b2(sher phyin): rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//rgyal ba la ni lha rgyal brgya byin gyis zhus pa//byang chub sems dpa' zhes rab spyod pa ji ltar brtson//phung po khams la rdul tsam brtson par mi byed 
7b3(sher phyin): cing //phung por mi brtson de ni byang chub sems dpa'i brtson//gang zhig chos 'di sprul dang rgyu ma 'drar thos nas//som nyi med cing slob la phyir zhing spyod byed pa//sems can de ni ring nas 
7b4(sher phyin): theg chen zhugs rig bya//sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig mang la lhag byas yin//dgon pa'i lam ni dpag tshad mang por zhugs pa'i mis//gnag rdzi mtshams kyi nags tshal phun sum tshogs 
7b5(sher phyin): mthong nas//'di dag grong dang grong khyer nye ba'i rtags snyam ste//dbugs phyin rnyed par 'gyur zhing chom kun 'jigs pa med//de bzhin byang chub yongs su tshol tshe rgyal rnams kyi//shes rab pha 
7b6(sher phyin): rol phyin 'di mnyan pa gang thob pa//de ni dbugs phyin thob par gyur cing 'jigs med de//dgra bcom sa la ma yin rkyen rtogs sa la min//mi zhig rgya mtsho'i chu ni blta phyir 'gro ba yis//
7b7(sher phyin): gal te shing dang nags tshal ri mthong da dung ring //ci ste rtags ma mthong na rgya mtsho chen po ni//nye bar gyur to snyam ste de la som nyi med//de ltar byang chub dam par rab zhugs rgyal ba yi//shes 
7b8(sher phyin): rab pha rol phyin pa 'di mnyan rig par bya//rnam par 'dren pas mngon sum lung bstan ma gyur kyang //sangs rgyas byang chub ring por mi thogs bdag reg 'gyur//dpyid dus bzang la sdong po

8b1(sher phyin): glang po rnyed nas glang po'i rjes ni tshol ba ltar//shes rab pha rol phyin thos mdo tshol de bzhin no//dper na la las ro brgya ldan pa'i zas rnyed la//zas mchog rnyed nas zas ngan tshol bar byed pa

8b3(sher phyin): kyis khyim 'dris byed ldan zhing //chos btang nas ni chos min bya ba spyod 'gyur ba//lam bor lam gol 'gro ba 'di ni bdud kyi las//gang dag de yi tshe na 'dun cing dad gyur nas//chos kyi 
8b4(sher phyin): dam pa 'di ni nyan du song ba las//de dag gis ni chos smra bya ba ldan rig nas//dga' ba med cing yid mi bde bar gyur nas 'gro//de yi tshe na bdud kyi las 'di 'byung bar 'gyur//de tshe gang dag 
8b5(sher phyin): gis ni dge slong mang dkrugs nas//shes rab pha rol phyin 'di 'dzin par mi 'gyur ba'i//bar du gcod pa rnam mang gzhan yang 'byung bar 'gyur//rin chen rin thang med pa rnyed 'gyur gang dag yin//
8b6(sher phyin): de dag dkon la dus rnams rtag tu 'tshe ba'ang mang //de bzhin rgyal ba'i shes rab pha rol phyin pa mchog/rin chen chos kyang dkon la rtag tu 'tshe ba'ang mang //theg pa gsar du zhugs pa'i sems 
8b7(sher phyin): can blo chung ba//gang zhig dkon pa'i rin chen 'di ni mi rnyed pa//de la bar chad bya phyir bdud ni spro bar 'gyur//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas de dag yongs su 'dzin la sbyor//ma zhig na bar gyur la bu ni 
8b8(sher phyin): mang yod pa//de kun yid mi bde zhing de la rim gro byed//de bzhin phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams kyi sangs rgyas kyang //yum gyur shes rab dam pa 'di la dgongs pa mdzad//gang dag 'das dang phyogs 
9a1(sher phyin): bcu'i 'jig rten mgon gang dang //ma 'ongs dus na 'byung 'gyur de dag 'di las 'byung //'jig rten ston pa rgyal ba rnams kyi skyed byed yum//sems can gzhan gyi sems kyi spyod pa ston 
9a2(sher phyin): par byed//'jig rten de bzhin nyid dang dgra bcom de bzhin nyid//rang rgyal de bzhin nyid dang rgyal sras de bzhin nyid//gcig pa nyid de dngos bral gzhan min de bzhin nyid//shes rab pha rol phyin pa de 
9a3(sher phyin): bzhin gshegs pas mkhyen//mkhas pa 'jig rten gnas sam mya ngan 'das kyang rung //chos nyid skyon med chos rnams stong pa 'di gnas te//byang chub sems dpas de bzhin nyid 'di rjes su rtogs//
9a4(sher phyin): de phyir de bzhin gshegs zhes sangs rgyas mtshan gsol to//shes rab pha rol phyin dga' tshal la rten pa//stobs bcu rnam 'dren rnams kyi spyod yul 'di yin te//sems can sdug bsngal ngan song 
9a5(sher phyin): gsum las yongs 'don kyang //de dag nam yang sems can 'du shes 'gyur ba med//dper na seng ge'i ri sul rten nas 'jigs med par//ri dgas phra mo mang skrag byed cing sgra sgrogs ltar//mi yi seng 
9a6(sher phyin): ge shes rab pha rol phyin brten nas//mu stegs can mang skrag byed 'jig rten sgra yang sgrogs//dper na nam mkhar gnas pa'i nyi ma'i 'od zer ni//sa 'di skems par byed cing gzugs kyang rab tu ston//
9a7(sher phyin): de bzhin chos rgyal shes rab pha rol phyin rten pa//sred pa'i chu klung skems shing chos kyang rab tu ston//gzugs rnams mi mthong tshor ba dag kyang mi mthong zhing //'du shes mthong ba med la sems pa 
9a8(sher phyin): mi mthong zhing //gang la rnam par shes dang sems yid mthong med pa//'di ni chos mthong yin zhes de bzhin gshegs pas bstan//nam mkha' mthong zhes sems can tshig tu rab brjod pa//
9b1(sher phyin): nam mkha' ji ltar mthong ste don 'di brtag par bgyi//de ltar chos mthong ba yang de bzhin gshegs pas bstan//mthong ba dpe gzhan gyis ni bsnyad par nus ma yin//gang gis de ltar mthong ba 
9b2(sher phyin): de yis chos kun mthong /rgyal po btang snyoms 'dug cing blon po kun byed ltar//sangs rgyas mdzad dang nyan thos chos rnams ji snyed pa//de dag thams cad shes rab pha rol phyin pa byed//rgyal po 
9b3(sher phyin): grong du mi 'gro yul 'khor mi 'gro la//rang gi yul nas 'du ba thams cad sdud pa ltar//byang chub sems dpa'i chos nyid gang du'ang mi 'gro la//sangs rgyas sa yi yon tan gang yin thams cad 
9b4(sher phyin): sdud//gang la bde gshegs byang chub sems dpa' dang brtan yod//shes rab pha rol phyin pa mchog la bsam sbyor ba//nyan thos rang rgyal sa gnyis shin du 'das byas nas//zil gyis mi gnon 
9b5(sher phyin): rgyal ba'i byang chub myur du thob//dper na rgya mtshor 'jug pa'i gru ni chag gyur la//mi gang ro'am rtswa'am shing ni mi 'dzin pa//chu dbus 'jig par 'gyur te ngogs su phyin mi 'gyur//gang zhig 
9b6(sher phyin): 'dzin pa rgya mtsho'i pha rol phyin par 'gyur//de ltar dad ldan dad pa thob pa gang zhig ni//shes rab pha rol phyin yum rjes su mi 'jug min//de dag thabs kyi yon tan shes rab yongs zin na//
9b7(sher phyin): mchog tu rmad byung bde gshegs byang chub myur du reg/dper na la las rdza so ma btang chu bskyed na//nyam chung phyir ni myur du 'jig par rig par bya//so btang bum par chu skyed lam du 'jig pa yi//
9b8(sher phyin): 'jigs pa med cing bde bar khyim du phyin par 'gyur//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' dad pa mang gyur kyang //shes rab rnam par nyams na myur du ma rung 'gyur//dad pas shes rab kyis ni yongs su 
10a1(sher phyin): @#//zin gyur na//sa gnyis shin tu 'das te byang chub mchog thob 'gyur//dper na bcos legs ma byas gru ni rgya mtsho'i nang //nor dang bcas shing tshong par bcas te 'jig par 'gyur//gru de legs par 
10a2(sher phyin): bcos legs byas dang ldan gyur na//mi 'jig nor dang bcas par dogs su phyin par 'gyur//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' dad pas yongs bsgos kyang //shes rab med na byang chub las ni myur nyams 
10a3(sher phyin): 'gyur//de nyid shes rab pha rol phyin pa mchog ldan na//ma smras ma nyams rgyal ba'i byang chub reg par 'gyur//lo brgya nyi shu lon te rgas shing sdug bsngal mi//langs kyang bdag nyid 'gro bar nus pa ma 
10a4(sher phyin): yin te//de ni g.yas g.yon gnyis nas mi yis rten byas na//'gyel ba'i 'jigs pa med cing bde blag 'gro bar 'gyur//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' shes rab stobs chung ba//rab tu zhugs par gyur 
10a5(sher phyin): kyang rab tu nyams par 'gyur//de nyid thabs dang shes rab mchog gis yongs zin na//mi nyams de bzhin gshegs kyi byang chub reg par 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa' dang po'i las kyi sar gnas gang //
10a6(sher phyin): lhag pa'i bsam pas sangs rgyas byang chub mchog zhugs pa//slob ma bzang po bla ma gus ldan de dag gis//bla ma mkhas pa rnams la rtag du brten par bya//ci phyir zhe na mkhas pa'i yon tan de las 
10a7(sher phyin): 'byung //de dag shes rab pha rol phyin pa rjes su ston//sangs rgyas chos rnams dge ba'i bshes la rten to zhes//yon tan kun gyi mchog mnga' rgyal ba de skad gsung //sbyin dang tshul khrims 
10a8(sher phyin): bzod dang de bzhin brtson 'grus dang //bsam gtan shes rab byang chub yongs su bsngo bar gyis//byang chub phung por zhen nas mchog 'dzin ma byed ces//dang po'i las can de la de skad bstan 
10b1(sher phyin): bar bya//de ltar spyod pa legs mtsho smra ba'i zla ba rnams//'gro ba'i skyabs dang dpung gnyen dang ni gnas yin te//rten dang blo dang gling dang yongs 'dren don 'dod pa//gsal byed sgron ma chos mchog 
10b2(sher phyin): smra ba mi 'khrugs yin//grags chen ldan rnams bya dka' go cha gyon byed de//phung po khams dang skyed mched rnams kyi go cha min//theg gsum 'du shes bral zhing yongs su bzung ba med//
10b3(sher phyin): mi ldog g.yo ba med cing mi 'khrugs chos can yin//de dag 'di 'dra'i chos dang ldan zhing spros pa med//the tshom som nyi yid gnyis bral zhing don dang ldan//shes rab pha rol phyin pa thob nas 
10b4(sher phyin): mi nur ga/gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog cing mi ldog rig par bya//'dren pa rnams kyi chos 'di zab ste mthong bar dka'//sus kyang rtogs pa med cing thob par 'gyur med pa//de phyir phan mdzad 
10b5(sher phyin): brtser ldan byang chub brnyes nas ni//sems can tshogs sus shes snyam thugs khral chung bar mdzad//sems can rnams ni gnas la dga' zhing yul rnams 'dod//'dzin la gnas 
10b6(sher phyin): shing mi mkhas blun rmongs mun pa bzhin//thob par bya ba'i chos ni gnas med 'dzin med de//des na 'jig rten dag dang rtsod pa 'byung ba yin//nam mkha'i khams ni shar gyi phyogs dang lho 
10b7(sher phyin): phyogs dang //de bzhin nub kyi phyogs dang byang phyogs pha mtha' yas//steng dang 'og dang phyogs bcu'i ji snyed rnams na 'ang yod//tha dad gyur pa med cing bye brag gyur pa med//'das pa'i 
10b8(sher phyin): de bzhin nyid gang ma byon de bzhin nyid//da ltar de bzhin nyid gang dgra bcom de bzhin nyid//chos kun de bzhin nyid gang rgyal ba'i de bzhin nyid//chos kyi de bzhin nyid 'di thams cad bye brag med//
11a1(sher phyin): @#//bde gshegs byang chub tha dad chos dang bral gyur pa//'di ni byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig thob 'dod pa//thabs dang ldan pas shes rab pha rol phyin la sbyor//'dren pa'i shes 
11a2(sher phyin): rab med na thob par 'gyur ma yin//bya zhig lus che dpag tshad brgya rtsa lnga bcu par//gyur la 'dab gshog zad de nyam rtsal yod min na//de yis sum bcu rtsa gsum gnas nas 'dzam gling 'dir//
11a3(sher phyin): bdag nyid mchongs na der ni nyam nyes gyur pa yin//rgyal ba rnams kyi pha rol phyin pa lnga 'di yang //skal ba bye ba khrag khrig mang por sgrub byas shing //smon lam mtha' yas rgya chen 'jig rten 
11a4(sher phyin): rtag bsten kyang //thabs med shes rab bral ba nyan thos nyid du ltung //sangs rgyas theg pa 'di la nges par 'byung 'dod gang //'gro ba kun la sems snyoms pha ma'i 'du shes dang //pham pa'i sems 
11a5(sher phyin): dang byams pa'i yid kyis brdul bya zhing //thabs med cing drang la tshig 'jam smra bar bya//'jig rten mgon la gnas brtan rab 'byor yongs zhus pa//yon tan rgya mtsho'i nyon mongs ma 
11a6(sher phyin): mchis rtags bstan gsol//mthu che bdag ji ltar slar ldog mi 'gyur ba//de ltar yon tan phyogs tsam rgyal bas lung bstan gsol//tha dad 'du shes bral zhing rigs pa ldan tshig smra//
11a7(sher phyin): dge spyod dag dang bram ze gzhan la rten mi byed//mkhas pas dus rnams rtag tu ngan song gsum po spangs//de dag dge bcu'i las kyi lam la mngon par brtson//zang zing med par 'gro la chos 
11a8(sher phyin): ni rjes su ston//chos la gcig tu dga' zhing rtag tu tshig 'jam ldan//'gro 'chag nyal dang 'dug par shes bzhin rab tu ldan//gnyal shing gang tsam lta zhing 'gro la sems 'khrul med//
11b1(sher phyin): gtsang byas gtsang ma'i gos 'chang gsum po dpen zhing dag/rnyed 'dod ma yin khyu mchog rtag tu chos 'dod pa//bdud kyi yul las 'das shing gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog/bsam gtan bzhi la bsam byed 
11b2(sher phyin): bsam gtan der mi gnas//grags pa 'dod pa ma yin khro bas sems dkris med//khyim par gyur kyang rtag tu dngos po kun ma chags//'tsho ba'i phyir ni yangs longs spyod tshol mi byed//drag shul spyod 
11b3(sher phyin): sngags mi byed bud med sbyor sngags min//'dod pa'i las can pho mor 'gyur zhes ston mi byed//rab tu dben pa'i shes rab pha rol phyin mchog brtson//'thab dang rtsod dang bral zhing byams pa'i 
11b4(sher phyin): sems kyang bstan//kun mkhyen 'dod cing brtan la rtag tu gzhol sems ldan//mtha' 'khob kla klo'i skye bo'i yul mtha' rnam par spangs//rang gi sa la dogs med rtag tu ri rab 'dra//chos 
11b5(sher phyin): phyir srog kyang 'dor zhing rnal 'byor rab tu brtson//mi ldog rtags ni 'di dag yin par shes par bya//gzugs dang tshor dang 'du shes sems pa rnam shes rnams//zab ste rang bzhin mtshan ma 
11b6(sher phyin): med cing rab zhi ba//dper na mda' yis rgya mtsho'i gting thug tshol ba ltar//shes rab kyis ni brtags na phung po'i gting mi rnyed//byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig de ltar zab gyur cing //theg 
11b7(sher phyin): pa don dam chags med gang la phung po dang //khams dang skye mched yod min chos 'di rtogs pa de'i//bsod nams yang dag grub pa de las ci zhig yod//dper na 'dod chags chos la spyod pa'i 
11b8(sher phyin): skyes bu zhig/bud med dus btab de dang ma phrad de yi ni//nyin cig sems kyi rnam rtog spyod pa ji snyed pa//de snyed bskal ba'i byang chub sems dpas thob par 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa'
12a1(sher phyin): @#//gang zhig bskal mang bye stong du//dgra bcom rang rgyal sbyin byed tshul khrims srung byed kyang //gang zhig shes rab pha rol phyin mchog ldan pa'i chos//brjod pa'i legs la sbyin dang tshul 
12a2(sher phyin): khrims char mi phod//byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig shes rab mchog bsgoms te//de las langs nas gos pa med pa'i chos brjod cing //de yang 'gro ba'i don phyir byang chub rgyur bsngo na//
12a3(sher phyin): dge ba de dang mnyam pa 'jig rten gsum na med//bsod nams de yang gsog dang de bzhin stong pa dang //gsob dang ya ma brla dang snying po med par shes//de ltar bde gshegs rnams

12a5(sher phyin): bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig mang por brjod byas kyang //chos kyi dbyings la zad pa med cing 'phel ba med//rgyal ba rnams kyi pha rol phyin ces bya ba gang //chos de rnams kyang ming tsam du ni 
12a6(sher phyin): yongs su bsgrags//yongs bsngo rlom sems med pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni//nyams pa med cing sangs rgyas byang chub mchog reg 'gyur//'bru mar snying po 'bar ba phrad pa dang po yis//
12a7(sher phyin): snying po tshig min de med par yang de mi tshig/me lce phrad pa thams snying po tshig min la//me lce tha ma med par snying po tshig min ltar//dang po'i sems kyis byang chub mchog reg ma yin la//de med par 
12a8(sher phyin): yang de la reg par nus ma yin//tha ma'i sems kyis byang chub zhi ba thob min la//de med par yang de thob nus pa ma yin no//sa bon las ni myu gu me tog 'bras bu 'byung //de yang 'gags la shing 
12b1(sher phyin): yang med pa der smin ltar//de bzhin dang po'i sems kyang byang chub rgyu yin te//de yang 'gags la byang chub med pa de ma yin//chu yi thigs pas bum pa dang po tha ma'i bar//rim gyis nyung ngu nyung 
12b2(sher phyin): ngus de yongs 'gengs pa ltar//de bzhin dang po sems kyang byang chub mchog rgyu ste//rim gyis dkar po'i yon tan rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'gyur//stong pa mtshan med smon pa med pa'i chos 
12b3(sher phyin): spyod cing //mya ngan 'das la mi reg mtshan mar mi spyod pa//dper na gnyan pa mkhas pa phar 'gro tshur 'gro yang //mtha' gnyis mi gnas rgya mtshor gnas pa med pa bzhin//de ltar spyod pa'i 
12b4(sher phyin): byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam bdag/stobs bcus lung bstan byang chub reg 'gyur rlom sems med//'di la ci yang med ces byang chub skrag pa med//de ltar spyod pa bde gshegs shes 
12b5(sher phyin): rab spyod pa yin//'jig rten mgon pa'i lam dang mu ge nad bcas par//mthong nas skrag pa med cing de nas go gyon te//phyi ma'i mu mthar rtag tu brtson zhing rab shes nas//yongs su skyo ba'i 
12b6(sher phyin): yid ni rdul tsam skye mi byed//byang chub sems dpa' rgyal ba'i shes rab spyod byed cing //phung po 'di dag gdod nas stong zhing skye med shes//mnyam par ma bzhag sems can khams la snying

13a1(sher phyin): lam du phyin pa dang //de yis dpa' zhing rtul phong skyes bu mang sprul te//bde bar song zhing phyir yang khyim du 'ong ba ltar//de bzhin de tshe byang chub sems dpa' mkhas pa yang //

13a3(sher phyin): chu'i phung po rten//de la sa chen 'di rten de la 'gro ba rten//sems can las la spyod pa'i rgyu ni de 'dra ste//nam mkha' gang la gnas yin don 'di bsam par gyis//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa'

13a6(sher phyin): snang 'phur ba'i bya la gnas yod ma yin te//de na gnas pa ma yin sa la ltung mi 'gyur//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' rnam thar sgor spyod pa//mya ngan 'das la mi rig mtshan mar spyod ma

13a8(sher phyin): 'dod na mda' de sa la ltung bar 'gyur//de ltar shes rab pha rol phyin pa mchog spyod pa//shes rab thabs stobs rdzu 'phrul gyis ni rnam spyod pa//de dag dge ba'i rtsa ba ji srid ma rdzogs pa//de 
13b1(sher phyin): srid stong nyid dam pa de ni thob mi byed//dper na dge slong rdzu 'phrul stobs mchog ldan pa zhig/nam mkhar gnas te cig char cho 'phrul rnam par 'phrul//'gro dang 'chag pa dang ni nyal dang 'dug 
13b2(sher phyin): pa bston//de ni skyo ba med cing de la dub pa med//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' mkhas pa stong gnas shing //ye shes rdzu 'phrul pha rol son la gnas pa med//'gro ba dag la bya ba rnam pa

13b4(sher phyin): par byed cing g.yang sa chen por lus bor nas//ltung bar mi 'gyur ji srid der ni 'gro bar 'gyur//de bzhin byang chub sem dpa' mkhas pa snying rjer gnas//thabs dang shes rab 
13b5(sher phyin): gnyis kyi gdugs ni yongs bzung ste//chos rnams stong pa mtshan med smon pa med rtog cing //mya ngan 'das la reg pa med la chos kyang mthong //dper na rin chen 'dod pa rin chen

14a1(sher phyin): chen gling du'ang mi gnas la//rig pa khyim du mi gnas lam la mkhas par 'gyur//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' gal te nyan thos dang //rang rgyal rnams kyi shes pa rnam

14a4(sher phyin): pa'i mi lus mi snang ma yin zhing //de ni ming gis kyang ni gdags par nus pa ltar//de bzhin byang chub se mas dpa' rnam thar sgor spyod pa//de ni ming gis kyang na gdags par nus pa yin 
14a5(sher phyin): gal te spyod pa dang ni dpar po yongs dris la//byang chub sems dpa' stong pa mtshan ma med pa'i chos//yongs su mi ston phyir mi ldog pas yi chos//mi 'chad de ni lung ma bstan par 
14a6(sher phyin): rig par bya//dgra bcom sa dang rkyen gyis sangs rgyas ye shes dang //khams gsum dag la rmi lam na yang mi 'dod cing //sangs rgyas rnams mthong 'gro la chos kyang ston byed pa//

14a8(sher phyin): kyis me yi phung po zhi//de ni mi ldog lung bstan min par rig par bya//mi yi 'jig rten 'byung po'i gdon dang nang mang ba//phan dang snying rtser ldan pa'i bden pa'i byin gyis zhi//'on kyang 
14b1(sher phyin): rlom sems med cing nga rgyal skye med pa//de ni mi ldog lung bstan yin par rig par bya//ci ste de ni bden pa'i byin rlabs sna tshogs dag/'byor bas bdag lung bstan snyam rlom sems 
14b2(sher phyin): 'byung ba'am//gal te byang chub sems dpa' gzhan gyis lung bstan rlom//rlom sems gnas pa de ni blo chung shes par bya//ming gi gzhi las bdud ni nye bar 'ongs gyur nas//

15a2(sher phyin): dam 'on te dgon par gnas kyang rung //theg pa gnyis kyi sems bral byang chub mchog des na//'di ni 'gro don bzhugs pa rnams kyis dben pa ste//byang chub sems dpa' drod nyom de ni 
15a3(sher phyin): bdag nyid nyams//de lta bas na byang chub dam pa tshol ba yis//bsam pa drag ldan mkhas pas nga rgyal nges bcom ste//nad pa'i tshogs kyis gso phyir sman pa sten pa ltar//g.yel ba med

15b1(sher phyin): dper na dogs pa'i 'du shes kyis ni dug bslang pa//dug de khong du song ba med kyang brgyal bar 'gyur//de bzhin byis pa bdag dang bdag gir khas len pa//bdag der 'du shes yang dag
